# About this item

*    Complete 23-Piece Oil Filter Kit: Includes 21 cup-type oil filter sockets covering 65–101mm (14 sizes), a 1/2" drive ratchet wrench, and a ring spanner for quick removal and installation of cartridge-style oil filter caps 
*    Fits Many Popular Vehicles: Compatible with select Toyota, VW, BMW, GMC Sierra, Ford, and Volvo models; designed for cartridge-style oil filter caps, not spin-on filters; confirm your filter cap diameter before ordering 
*    Socket-Style Grip Design: Precision-machined cups fit snugly over the filter cap to reduce slipping and help protect the housing from damage, even when removing tightly installed caps 
*    Durable Aluminum Construction: Forged from heavy-duty aluminum with a black anodized finish for strength, corrosion resistance, and lightweight handling during repeated oil changes 
*    Clear Size Markings And Easy Use: Each socket is permanently marked with the metric size and model number for quick selection; works with the included 1/2" ratchet or ring spanner for versatile access in tight engine bays

### Customers say

Customers find the oil filter wrench set to be of good quality and appreciate its functionality, with one customer noting it includes a 3/8" ratchet and thin wrench. The durability and stability receive mixed feedback - while some find it sturdy, others report it breaking on first use and slipping when force is applied. The fit and cap sizes also get mixed reviews, with some saying it fits every size while others report it doesn't work with euro cars 96 and up, and while many appreciate the variety of sizes, some find them too small. The value for money receives mixed opinions, with some considering it worth the price while others say it's not worth the money.

## About this Item

*   Complete 23-Piece Oil Filter Kit: Includes 21 cup-type oil filter sockets covering 65–101mm (14 sizes), a 1/2" drive ratchet wrench, and a ring spanner for quick removal and installation of cartridge-style oil filter caps
*   Fits Many Popular Vehicles: Compatible with select Toyota, VW, BMW, GMC Sierra, Ford, and Volvo models; designed for cartridge-style oil filter caps, not spin-on filters; confirm your filter cap diameter before ordering
*   Socket-Style Grip Design: Precision-machined cups fit snugly over the filter cap to reduce slipping and help protect the housing from damage, even when removing tightly installed caps
*   Durable Aluminum Construction: Forged from heavy-duty aluminum with a black anodized finish for strength, corrosion resistance, and lightweight handling during repeated oil changes
*   Clear Size Markings And Easy Use: Each socket is permanently marked with the metric size and model number for quick selection; works with the included 1/2" ratchet or ring spanner for versatile access in tight engine bays

## Product Description

 Description： 
BETOOLL is well-known as a leader in providing quality auto parts to the aftermarket. BETOOLL has earned the reputation for excellence from over five years of experience in providing automotive replacement parts primarily for the automotive aftermarket. The prestigious position stems from a unique combination of application expertise, innovative product design, and breadth of product offerings, many of which are not conveniently or economically available elsewhere.

Package included: 1pc Ratchet. 1pc Ring Spanner. 21pcs Oil Filter Cups: 901 65m/m p-14 902 67m/m p-14 903 74m/m p-14 903-1 75m/m p-15 903-2 74m/m p-15 903-3 74m/m p-15 903-4 73m/m p-14 903-6 75m/m p-18 903-7 74m/m p-15 904 79m/m p-15 905 73m/m p-15 905-1 74m/m p-14 906 84m/m p-18 907 89m/m p-15 908 91m/m p-15 909 82m/m p-15 910 95m/m p-15 911 76m/m p-12 911-1 79m/m p-15 915 92m/m p-15 914 101m/m p-15
